- **atomic-commit**: - 代码修改完成，需要提交 - 文档变更完成，需要提交 - 配置更新完成，需要提交 - 功能开发完成，需要提交 - 修复问题完成，需要提交 ([xinetzone/SpecWeave](https://github.com/xinetzone/SpecWeave/blob/HEAD/.agents/commands/atomic-commit.md) · ★2)
- **atomic-commit**: - [ ] This commit addresses exactly one logical change - [ ] The commit message does NOT contain the word "also" - [ ] Commit message follows format: `type(scope): description` - [ ] Type is one of: feat, fix, test, refactor, docs, chore - [ ] All tests pass - [ ] This commit could be reverted without affecting any other concern ([pmurasky/argus](https://github.com/pmurasky/argus/blob/HEAD/.opencode/commands/atomic-commit.md) · ★1)
- **atomic-commit**: 변경 사항을 분석하여 의미 있는 단위로 분리하고 Conventional Commit 형태로 커밋 ([m16khb/cc-essential](https://github.com/m16khb/cc-essential/blob/HEAD/commands/atomic-commit.md))
- **atomic-commit**: Create small, focused atomic commits. Use when committing changes. ([MaceGrim/claude-code-config](https://github.com/MaceGrim/claude-code-config/blob/HEAD/commands/atomic-commit.md))
- **atomic-commit**: Split the working tree into atomic Conventional Commits and commit them ([wall3n/dotfiles](https://github.com/wall3n/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/claude/commands/atomic-commit.md))
- **atomic-commits**: Analyze staged changes and organize them into intuitive atomic commits following conventional commits. ([spencermarx/open-code-review](https://github.com/spencermarx/open-code-review/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/atomic-commits.md) · ★388 · 3 copies)

Groups diffs by logical feature, validates each group independently, and commits in dependency order. ([guilhermeUpToTask/praxis-orchestrator](https://github.com/guilhermeUpToTask/praxis-orchestrator/blob/HEAD/.mimocode/commands/atomic-commits.md) · ★1)

- **atomic-help**: Route a lost user to the right atomic verb, skill, agent, or binary subcommand. Bare invocation reads git state and recommends one next step. Topic keyword or freeform intent → focused pointer. `tour` → guided walkthrough of the system. Help router, not duplicated docs. ([damusix/atomic-claude](https://github.com/damusix/atomic-claude/blob/HEAD/context/commands/atomic-help.md) · ★117)
- **atomic-plan**: Write a design doc (concepts, business rules, approaches) and a checkpoint-table spec (contract) for non-trivial work; inline spec only for trivial. ([damusix/atomic-claude](https://github.com/damusix/atomic-claude/blob/HEAD/context/commands/atomic-plan.md) · ★117)
